- **mantenimiento**: Use this agent when performing maintenance tasks such as system updates, log cleanup, backup verification, or other routine upkeep operations. ([jvchiappini/DesignStudio](https://github.com/jvchiappini/DesignStudio/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/mantenimiento.md) · ★1)
- **mantine-ux**: Use for any UI/UX work in this Mantine 9 + React 19 operator-admin app: planning a new panel/screen, choosing Mantine components and layout, or reviewing implemented UI against operator-grade dark-UX standards. - **mantis-architect**: Heavy reasoning for MANTIS AI (AlgoTrader). - **mantis-implementer**: Implementation executor for MANTIS AI (AlgoTrader). - **manuscript-reviewer-methodology**: Sharp review — methodological rigor + reproducibility. Hidden assumptions, proof gaps, definitional sloppiness, plus missing code/data/hyperparameters/seeds. ([DawnEver/cc-market](https://github.com/DawnEver/cc-market/blob/HEAD/cc-academia/agents/manuscript-reviewer-methodology.md) · ★4)
- **manuscript-reviewer-novelty**: Sharp review — novelty + related-work coverage.
